{"forms":[{"form":"the consensus theorem","tags":["canonical"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"name","senses":[{"glosses":["The following theorem of Boolean algebra: XY+X'Z+YZ=XY+X'Z where YZ, the algebraically redundant term, is called the \"consensus term\", or its dual form (X+Y)(X'+Z)(Y+Z)=(X+Y)(X'+Z), in which case Y+Z is the consensus term. (Note: X+Y,X'+Z⊢Y+Z is an example of the resolution inference rule (replacing the + with ∨ and the prime with prefix ¬ might make this more evident).)"],"topics":["human-sciences","logic","mathematics","philosophy","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":3692527,"upstream_offset":9402939762,"upstream_record_sha256":"c55ec00aba36954aa9d7c229480e1a88f34e6a0a13e3966f68dc88f11686df5e","word":"consensus theorem"}

{"forms":[{"form":"euhemerizations","tags":["plural"]},{"form":"euhemerisation","tags":["alternative","UK"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The euhemerization of Taoist myth by Confucian historians sometimes led to the appearance of figures such as \"Lord Primordial Chaos\" (渾沌氏) in the imperial genealogies.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The fanciful invention of plausible historical figures and events as an attempt to rationalize mythology and legends"],"tags":["derogatory","uncountable"]},{"examples":[{"text":"One such euhemerization is a Rowan Atkinson bit where he presents Jesus Christ as a misunderstood street magician.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The fanciful invention of plausible historical figures and events as an attempt to rationalize mythology and legends","an instance of this"],"tags":["countable","derogatory"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Much of Chinese mythology appears to have been the result of euhemerization, the later deification of Neolithic tribal leaders and inventors incompletely remembered through oral traditions and now-lost records.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Synonym of deification: the actual creation of mythology and legends from historical figures and events; (countable) an instance of this."],"synonyms":[{"tags":["synonym","synonym-of"],"word":"deification"}],"tags":["uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/juˌhiməɹɪzˈeɪʃən/","tags":["General-American"]},{"ipa":"/juːˌhiːməɹɪzˈeɪʃən/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"fanciful creation of rational explanations for the supernatural","word":"rationalization & pseudohistory"}],"upstream_line":3509557,"upstream_offset":9102944917,"upstream_record_sha256":"9af2a405b9ec9cbc4537e675c14770e36f162b8bbbacdd71f6fa799c636b25b7","word":"euhemerization"}
